### DEVT16  (moved from main-lane DEVT15 — sliced: target-matching only)

'No debug target for one tab, but it should have' — a WebEngine tab renders as non-debuggable/disabled in the BiDi context selector. Audit DEVT10's tab-vs-target matching: the strip enumeration pairs Arora tabs to CDP targets — likely an off-by-one/index mismatch (tab N matched to target N-1), a stale getTargets snapshot taken before the tab's target registered, or a real WebEngine page whose target.type isn't exactly 'page' (check prerender/devtools/portal types). The fix: re-match correctly, and when a WebEngine tab has no CDP target YET, mark it 'attaching…' and retry once targets refresh rather than permanently 'no debug target'. Verify: BiDi open on a real page → every WebEngine tab shows selectable in the combo (non-WebEngine = disabled-with-reason only). make check PASS.

DONE (2026-10-16): root cause was not index-matching (claims were always url-based, so no off-by-one) but the stale-snapshot + strict-string pair: (a) the bridge forwards Target.targetInfoChanged as browsingContext.navigationStarted, which the panel consumed for footer timings only — a tab whose target committed a url after the last getTree kept mis-merging against the cached row; (b) the match demanded exact string equality while the two sides legitimately spell the same page differently (typed "http://h" vs committed "http://h/", pre-commit "" vs about:blank, chrome-error://chromewebdata/ on failed loads); (c) an unmatched web tab pinned "no debug target" permanently — nothing re-asked for the tree when the tab outran targetCreated.

- bidipanel.cpp: rebuildContextCombo split into claim/render passes — urlsMatch() normalizes (root "/" dropped since QUrl::StripTrailingSlash keeps it, empty/"about:blank" equivalence), a lone open web tab claims a lone chrome-error target positionally (ambiguous sets refuse to pair), and web tabs that end unmatched read "attaching…" while the channel is live; armAttachRetry() re-issues getTree up to 6x600ms (m_attachRetries resets on every browsingContext event / reconnect / strip rebind), "no debug target" returns once the budget is spent. navigationStarted patches the cached context url + re-merges, and an event for an unknown context escalates to a fresh getTree. type!="page" audit: Qt WebEngine tabs are always page targets — nothing to fix bridge-side.
- Verified: tst_devtools 15/15 incl new bidiContextRematch (attaching label + armed retry, navigationStarted re-merge, slash normalization, unique/ambiguous error pairing, verdict-after-budget); --bidi-smoke PASS offscreen --disable-gpu.

### MEM08

User request: a manual 'flush memory' item on the tab right-click menu as a workaround even if MEM06's automatic fix only partly helps. MEM05 established the retention lives in the renderer's per-navigation WebContents retention — the ONLY honest flush is tearing the WebContents down (history()->clear() freed 0 kB; verified by the bare-view control). SLEEP01 already has the discard machinery: TabBar Sleep/Wake entry -> TabWidget::sleepTab/beginTabSleep -> Engine::Page::setLifecycleState(Discarded) frees the renderer side while the slot keeps title+favicon.

Build: (a) add 'Free Memory' to the tab context menu next to Sleep/Wake. For a NON-current tab: run the existing discard path (beginTabSleep) — that IS the flush; same exemptions (form-dirty refusal) apply. For the CURRENT tab: sleeping is impossible, so the honest flush is discard+immediate reactivate — setLifecycleState(Discarded) then back to Active, which rebuilds the WebContents and reloads the current entry, dropping all retained session documents (user-visible effect = a reload; label it honestly, e.g. 'Reload && Free Memory' or one item whose text reflects the action). If discarding the active tab is not reliable, fall back to a WebContents recreate (reloadIntoNewContents-style) and document why. (b) Status feedback: sample the tab's renderer RSS via the same processMemoryKb(renderProcessId()) path the mem indicator uses before and ~2s after; show 'freed ~N MB' or 'no memory freed' in the statusbar — honest, no fake numbers; a sleeping tab shows 'already discarded'. (c) Optional window-level 'Free Memory in Background Tabs' in the same menu — sleeps all suspendable tabs; only add if it stays small. Verify: tabbar menu leg (item present, enabled/disabled states, current-vs-background wording); a memcycle-style leg asserting renderer RSS drops after flush on a tab that has cycled navigations; make check PASS.

RUNS IN PARALLEL with PROF02 (rust lane) and PROF03 (main lane) — design the menu so their items slot in without rework (see d).
